Q1. A stock is enough for 20 people for 24 days. Find its duration for 30 people.
A. 16 days
B. 13 days
C. 20 days
D. 11 days
Answer: 16 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_food_resource_basic
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
M_1D_1=M_2D_2
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
20\times 24=30\times D_2
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
D_2=16
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=16
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D_2=\frac{20\times 24}{30}
\]
Answer: 16 days

---

Q2. A and B begin a job together. After 3 days A leaves. If A and B alone take 24 and 10 days, find when the job ends.
A. 13.75 days
B. 6.75 days
C. 8.75 days
D. 10.5 days
Answer: 8.75 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_forward_leave
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
W=(r_A+r_B)t_1+r_Bt_2
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
W_1=(5+12)\times 3
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=3+\frac{69}{12}=8.75
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=8.75
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=3+\frac{W-W_1}{B}
\]
Answer: 8.75 days

---

Q3. A needs 20 days and B needs 18 days. In a joint job, A is absent for the last 2 days and B for the last 3 days. Find total duration.
A. 7 days
B. 12 days
C. 13 days
D. 9 days
Answer: 12 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_backward_leave
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
r_A(T-a)+r_B(T-b)=W
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
18(T-2)+20(T-3)=360
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=12
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W+A\times 2+B\times 3}{A+B}
\]
Answer: 12 days

---

Q4. Use net pipe rate for fill times 12 and emptying time none. Find the time.
A. 4 hours
B. 7 hours
C. 9 hours
D. 6.75 hours
Answer: 9 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_partial_tank_initially_filled
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
T=\frac{work\ left}{r_{fill}-r_{empty}}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
r=1/12
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=9
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=9
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=remaining/net\ rate
\]
Answer: 9 hours

---

Q5. Workers contribute in the ratio 15:24 to a ₹9000 job. Find the asked share.
A. ₹3467.54
B. ₹3461.54
C. ₹3464.54
D. ₹4326.92
Answer: ₹3461.54
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_wage_efficiency_ratio
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
Share=\frac{c_i}{\sum c}\times W
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
Share=\frac{15}{39}\times 9000
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
Share=3461.54
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=3461.54
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
Share=contribution\ ratio\times wage
\]
Answer: ₹3461.54

Q6. A job has 300 units. 8 workers finish 48 units, and 12 days are left. Find the extra workers required.
A. 9 workers
B. 15.6 workers
C. 13 workers
D. 10 workers
Answer: 13 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_worker_added_to_meet_deadline
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
extra=\left\lceil\frac{R}{r\times t}\right\rceil-current
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
extra=\left\lceil\frac{252}{1\times 12}\right\rceil-8
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
extra=13
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=13
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
extra=required-current
\]
Answer: 13 workers

---

Q7. 12 workers start a job of 300 units and work 8 days. Then 2 workers leave. Find the delay compared with the original plan.
A. 3.4 days
B. 0.5 days
C. 5.4 days
D. 4.25 days
Answer: 3.4 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_worker_removed_delay
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
D=T_{actual}-T_{planned}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
R=300-12\times 8
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
D=\frac{204}{10}-\frac{204}{12}
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=3.4
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D=T_a-T_p
\]
Answer: 3.4 days

Q8. A completes \(\frac{1}{3}\) of a work in 6 days. Find the time needed for the whole work.

\[

\[

\]

\[
T=18
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=18
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=6\div \frac{1}{3}=18
\]
Answer: 18 days

\[
W_A:W_B=10:6
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
W_A:W_B=5:3
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=5:3
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
E=5:3
\]
Answer: 5:3

Q15. A job needs 12 men for 15 days at 8 hours per day. Find the men required for 16 days at 6 hours per day.
A. 12.8 workers
B. 15 workers
C. 18 workers
D. 12 workers
Answer: 15 workers
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_man_days_hours_basic
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
M_1D_1H_1=M_2D_2H_2
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
12\times 15\times 8=M_2\times 16\times 6
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
M_2=15
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
M_2=\frac{12\times 15\times 8}{16\times 6}=15
\]
Answer: 15 workers

Q23. A filling pipe takes 12 hours. Because of a leak, the tank fills in 22 hours. Find the leak time.
A. 29.4 hours
B. 32.4 hours
C. 33 hours
D. 26.4 hours
Answer: 26.4 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_leak_hidden_rate
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{F}-\frac{1}{T}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{12}-\frac{1}{22}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
L=26.4
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=26.4
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
L=\frac{FT}{T-F}
\]
Answer: 26.4 hours

\[

Q27. A needs 30 days and B needs 18 days for the same work. After A works 2 days alone, both work together. Find the completion time from the start.
A. 16.5 days
B. 12.5 days
C. 18.75 days
D. 14.5 days
Answer: 12.5 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_delayed_join
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
W_1=r_At_1,\ T_2=\frac{W-W_1}{r_A+r_B}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
W_1=3\times 2,\quad R=90-6
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=2+\frac{84}{3+5}=12.5
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=12.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=2+\frac{90-3\times 2}{3+5}
\]
Answer: 12.5 days

Q30. The efficiencies of A and B are in the ratio 2:3. Together they finish the work in 6 days. Find the time taken by A alone.
A. 15 days
B. 11 days
C. 9 days
D. 4 days
Answer: 15 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_efficiency_ratio_scaling
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
W=(a+b)T
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
W=(2+3)\times 6
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T_A=\frac{30}{2}=15
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T_A=\frac{(a+b)T}{a}=\frac{(2+3)6}{2}=15
\]
Answer: 15 days

Q34. Pipe A fills a tank in 20 hours. Pipes A and B together fill it in 10 hours. Find the time taken by B alone.
A. 20 hours
B. 17 hours
C. 26 hours
D. 16 hours
Answer: 20 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_find_pipe_rate_from_net_time
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{10}-\frac{1}{20}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
B=20
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=20
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{AT}{A-T}
\]
Answer: 20 hours

Q35. If a job takes 12 days and 2 days have already been worked, what part is still unfinished?
A. 1/6
B. 6/5
C. 5/6
D. 5/1
Answer: 5/6

\[

\]

\[
R=1-\frac{2}{12}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
R=\frac{10}{12}=5/6
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=5/6
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
R=\frac{12-2}{12}=5/6
\]
Answer: 5/6

\[

Q37. Three pairwise teams complete the work in 10, 12 and 15 days respectively. Find A's individual time.
A. 29 days

D. 18 days
Answer: 24 days
Difficulty: Hard

Explanation:

\[

\[
AB=10,\ BC=12,\ CA=15
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T_A=24
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
A=\frac{AB+AC-BC}{2}
\]
Answer: 24 days

Q72. The combined time of A and B is 6 days. If A can finish alone in 18 days, find B's time.
A. 9 days
B. 14 days
C. 12 days
D. 24 days
Answer: 9 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_individual_from_combined
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{6}-\frac{1}{18}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
B=9
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=9
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{6\times 18}{18-6}=9
\]
Answer: 9 days

---

Q73. A job changes team after 4 days. A, B and C alone take 15, 20 and 18 days. If B is replaced by C, find the completion time.
A. 4.36 days
B. 10.04 days
C. 10.36 days
D. 8.36 days
Answer: 8.36 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_replacement_worker
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
r_1t_1+r_2t_2=W
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
W_1=21\times 4
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=4+\frac{96}{22}=8.36
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=8.36
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_2}
\]
Answer: 8.36 days

Therefore the required answer is obtained.

Ans=4
\]

\[
T=\frac{12\times 6}{12+6}=4
\]
Answer: 4 days

Q83. 10 men working 6 hours a day finish a work in 8 days. How many men are needed to finish it in 15 days working 5 hours a day?
A. 6.4 workers
B. 11.4 workers
C. 10 workers
D. 18.75 workers
Answer: 6.4 workers
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_farm_harvest_workers
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
M_1D_1H_1=M_2D_2H_2
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
10\times 8\times 6=M_2\times 15\times 5
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
M_2=6.4
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=6.4
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
M_2=\frac{10\times 8\times 6}{15\times 5}=6.4
\]
Answer: 6.4 workers

A. 16 workers
B. 11.52 workers
C. 26.67 workers
D. 15 workers
Answer: 11.52 workers
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_farm_harvest_workers
Explanation:

\[
M_1D_1H_1=M_2D_2H_2
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
16\times 12\times 6=M_2\times 20\times 5
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
M_2=11.52
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=11.52
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
M_2=\frac{16\times 12\times 6}{20\times 5}=11.52
\]
Answer: 11.52 workers

Answer: 5.33 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_delayed_join

\[

Substitute the given values.
\[

\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=2+\frac{10}{1+2}=5.33
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.

Ans=5.33
\]

\]

\[

\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.

Ans=5:12

\[

\]
Answer: 5:12

W=LCM(10,16)=80
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
A=80/10=8,\quad B=80/16=5
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=\frac{80}{8+5}=6.15
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=6.15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{10\times 16}{10+16}=6.15
\]
Answer: 6.15 days

D. 11.57 days
Answer: 11.57 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_interrupted_work
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
T_{calendar}=T_{active}+T_{idle}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
T_{active}=\frac{780}{52+39}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=T_{active}+3=11.57
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=11.57
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=active+idle
\]
Answer: 11.57 days

Q491. 720 person-days of food are available. 30 people consume it for 5 days, then the group becomes 36. Find how many more days the food will last.

B. 11.83 days
C. 15.83 days
D. 16.83 days
Answer: 15.83 days

Family: tw_food_remaining_stock
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
D=\frac{S-consumed}{people}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
D=\frac{720-30\times 5}{36}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
D=15.83
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=15.83
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D=remaining\ stock/final\ people
\]
Answer: 15.83 days

Q492. 10 workers have completed 100 units of a 240-unit job. Only 8 days remain. How many extra workers are needed?
A. 6 workers
B. 12 workers
C. 8 workers
D. 4 workers
Answer: 8 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_contract_deadline_extra_workers
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
extra=\left\lceil\frac{R}{r\times t}\right\rceil-current
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
extra=\left\lceil\frac{140}{1\times 8}\right\rceil-10
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
extra=8
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=8
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
extra=required-current
\]
Answer: 8 workers

Q494. A work is planned to finish in 18 days. After 8 days of work, find the remaining fraction.
A. 5/4
B. 5/9
C. 9/5
D. 4/9
Answer: 5/9
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_residual_work
Explanation:

\[
R=1-\frac{t}{T}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
R=1-\frac{8}{18}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
R=\frac{10}{18}=5/9
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=5/9
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
R=\frac{18-8}{18}=5/9
\]
Answer: 5/9

Q495. A alone needs 15 days, while A and B together need 8 days. How many days will B alone need?
A. 11.5 days
B. 7 days
C. 17.14 days
D. 23 days
Answer: 17.14 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_individual_from_combined
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{8}-\frac{1}{15}
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
B=17.14
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=17.14
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{8\times 15}{15-8}=17.14
\]
Answer: 17.14 days

Q496. A completes a job in 20 days, while B completes it in 12 days. Find the time required when both work together.
A. 16 days
B. 7.5 days
C. 32 days
D. 12 days
Answer: 7.5 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
W=LCM(20,12)=60
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
A=60/20=3,\quad B=60/12=5
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=\frac{60}{3+5}=7.5
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=7.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{20\times 12}{20+12}=7.5
\]
Answer: 7.5 days

Q497. A stock is enough for 24 people for 36 days. Find its duration for 25 people.
A. 30.56 days
B. 37.56 days
C. 51.84 days
D. 34.56 days
Answer: 34.56 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_food_resource_basic
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
M_1D_1=M_2D_2
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
24\times 36=25\times D_2
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
D_2=34.56
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=34.56
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D_2=\frac{24\times 36}{25}
\]
Answer: 34.56 days

Q498. A and B start a work together. A alone takes 18 days and B alone takes 6 days. A leaves after 3 days; B finishes the rest. Find the total time.
A. 0.5 days
B. 5 days
C. 3.75 days
D. 3 days
Answer: 5 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_forward_leave
Explanation:
Use the governing rate-state formula.
\[
W=(r_A+r_B)t_1+r_Bt_2
\]
Substitute the given values.
\[
W_1=(2+6)\times 3
\]
Simplify to get the required value.
\[
T=3+\frac{12}{6}=5
\]
Therefore the required answer is obtained.
\[
Ans=5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=3+\frac{W-W_1}{B}
\]
Answer: 5 days
